apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1151:CC-1 — `configTestCmd` 的坏配置语义未定义完整。  
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1152:req-v1 只说 `configTestCmd` 使用同一解析器、fence 内示例不再生效，但没有说明 `test-cmd` 重复异值、空值、或解析器 `problems` 出现时 `verify` / `gate` / `doctor` 各自如何 fail-closed。现在 `verify` 可省略 `--test-cmd` 走 config fallback；`gate` 和 `doctor D5` 也调用 `configTestCmd`。若坏配置只是返回 null，会和“无 test-cmd 配置”混在一起；若返回错误对象，三个消费面需要明确 exit/status。  
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1153:Fix：定义 `configTestCmd` 的结果契约，例如 `{value, problems}`；`verify` 配置错误 → exit 2 / JSON `ERROR`，不运行测试；`gate` 配置错误 → exit 2 `ERROR`，不运行 C1；`doctor` 配置错误 → D5 finding 还是 UNUSABLE 需明确。至少要具名错误并证明不会执行 fenced/commented/冲突 test-cmd。
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1156:req-v1 写“只识别 `| key | value |` 形状的表行”，但 `templates/process-config.md` 当前是四列表：`| Field | Value | Legal range | Default |`，`init --test-cmd` 也追加多列行。若严格按两列表解释，现有 `language`、未来 `cas`、`test-cmd` 都可能不被识别。  
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1159:CC-3 — 解析边界还有未决语义，尤其 fence/comment 异常形态与同值重复。  
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1160:req-v1 说 fence 与 spec-runner 一致，但 spec-runner 的 `stripFences` 是非行状态机的最短 regex，对未闭合 fence 不跳过到 EOF；这里又要求“逐行扫描”。HTML comment 也未说明未闭合注释是否跳到 EOF。另有“同值重复 → 取一并记 note 级 problem?” 与“同值重复容忍”混杂；如果 `problems` 触发 fail-closed，note 级 problem 会把本应容忍的情况变成错误。  
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1161:Fix：写死规则：fence 只认行首/可缩进三反引号，开启后到下一 fence 行，未闭合则到 EOF 均跳过；HTML `<!--` 到下一 `-->`，未闭合到 EOF 跳过；同值重复不进入 `problems`，可选进入 `notes`，异值重复才 problem。
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1171:`--no-cas` 高于坏 `cas` config 的设计合理，但建议限定为只压过 `cas` 键错误，不应压过 `test-cmd` 错误。
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1177:| CC-1 | `configTestCmd` bad-config behavior is underspecified for verify/gate/doctor. | Bad config may be treated as “missing config” or still run an unsafe fallback. | STEP0·r1 | open |
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1179:| CC-3 | Fence/comment edge semantics and same-value duplicate handling are ambiguous. | Implementations can diverge on unterminated blocks or fail tolerated duplicates. | STEP0·r1 | open |
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1187:CC-1 — `configTestCmd` 的坏配置语义未定义完整。  
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1188:req-v1 只说 `configTestCmd` 使用同一解析器、fence 内示例不再生效，但没有说明 `test-cmd` 重复异值、空值、或解析器 `problems` 出现时 `verify` / `gate` / `doctor` 各自如何 fail-closed。现在 `verify` 可省略 `--test-cmd` 走 config fallback；`gate` 和 `doctor D5` 也调用 `configTestCmd`。若坏配置只是返回 null，会和“无 test-cmd 配置”混在一起；若返回错误对象，三个消费面需要明确 exit/status。  
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1189:Fix：定义 `configTestCmd` 的结果契约，例如 `{value, problems}`；`verify` 配置错误 → exit 2 / JSON `ERROR`，不运行测试；`gate` 配置错误 → exit 2 `ERROR`，不运行 C1；`doctor` 配置错误 → D5 finding 还是 UNUSABLE 需明确。至少要具名错误并证明不会执行 fenced/commented/冲突 test-cmd。
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1192:req-v1 写“只识别 `| key | value |` 形状的表行”，但 `templates/process-config.md` 当前是四列表：`| Field | Value | Legal range | Default |`，`init --test-cmd` 也追加多列行。若严格按两列表解释，现有 `language`、未来 `cas`、`test-cmd` 都可能不被识别。  
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1195:CC-3 — 解析边界还有未决语义，尤其 fence/comment 异常形态与同值重复。  
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1196:req-v1 说 fence 与 spec-runner 一致，但 spec-runner 的 `stripFences` 是非行状态机的最短 regex，对未闭合 fence 不跳过到 EOF；这里又要求“逐行扫描”。HTML comment 也未说明未闭合注释是否跳到 EOF。另有“同值重复 → 取一并记 note 级 problem?” 与“同值重复容忍”混杂；如果 `problems` 触发 fail-closed，note 级 problem 会把本应容忍的情况变成错误。  
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1197:Fix：写死规则：fence 只认行首/可缩进三反引号，开启后到下一 fence 行，未闭合则到 EOF 均跳过；HTML `<!--` 到下一 `-->`，未闭合到 EOF 跳过；同值重复不进入 `problems`，可选进入 `notes`，异值重复才 problem。
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt:1207:`--no-cas` 高于坏 `cas` config 的设计合理，但建议限定为只压过 `cas` 键错误，不应压过 `test-cmd` 错误。

apriori/changes/config-contract/review/spec-review-v1-raw.txt:150:`archive`'s waiver lookup SHALL go through the shared structured config reader: fenced/commented rows grant nothing, a live row decides, and a cas CONFLICT or illegal value at consultation is a preflight error (exit 1, nothing written or moved) naming the config problem — bad config never equals a waiver; `--no-cas` keeps explicit supremacy; stamped or ADDED-only runs never consult the key, so a bad row cannot affect them.
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/spec-review-v1-raw.txt:159:### Requirement: process-config parses as structure, never as full-text regex
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/spec-review-v1-raw.txt:160:A shared reader (`lib/config.js`) SHALL be the single entry for every process-config consumer: it scans line-by-line, treating fenced code blocks (``` open/close; an unterminated fence makes the rest of the file inert) and HTML comments (`<!-- … -->`, multi-line; unterminated likewise inert; first-open wins, no nesting) as non-content; a config row is any `|`-leading table row — first cell key, second cell value, extra columns ignored, header and separator rows skipped; duplicate rows with the same key and value are tolerated silently, while different values for one key are a CONFLICT problem. Config problems surface ONLY when the key is actually consumed. The `--no-cas` flag keeps explicit supremacy over any config state.
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/spec-review-v1-raw.txt:162:#### Scenario: CF-01 fenced and commented rows never take effect
apriori/changes/config-contract/review/spec-review-v1-raw.txt:163:- WHEN process-config carries `| cas | optional |` inside a fenced block (or an HTML comment) and `| cas | required |` as a live row, with an unstamped MODIFIED delta being archived

ADDED-only deltas stay exempt (the exemption is clobber-focused: they conflict or no-op, never silently overwrite). `verify --change` (the read-only projection) keeps warning without judging; `buildProjection` still returns `unstampedMutations: string[]`. `merge()`'s MODIFIED op SHALL report `unchanged` when the delta block trim-equals the current store block. Archive preflight SHALL classify each stamped delta per file: stamp matches → merge as today; stamp mismatches with EVERY op `unchanged` → a note (`rerun accepted`) and the run proceeds, move included; stamp mismatches with ANY real pending op → error, the whole preflight fails with nothing written or moved — divergence with pending work is never repaired, and notes stay distinct from errors in the diagnostics.

apriori/changes/config-contract/review/spec-review-v1-raw.txt:392:apriori/changes/config-contract/specs/config/spec.md:5:A shared reader (`lib/config.js`) SHALL be the single entry for every process-config consumer: it scans line-by-line, treating fenced code blocks (``` open/close; an unterminated fence makes the rest of the file inert) and HTML comments (`<!-- … -->`, multi-line; unterminated likewise inert; first-open wins, no nesting) as non-content; a config row is any `|`-leading table row — first cell key, second cell value, extra columns ignored, header and separator rows skipped; duplicate rows with the same key and value are tolerated silently, while different values for one key are a CONFLICT problem. Config problems surface ONLY when the key is actually consumed. The `--no-cas` flag keeps explicit supremacy over any config state.

apriori/changes/config-contract/specs/config/spec.md:4:### Requirement: process-config parses as structure, never as full-text regex
apriori/changes/config-contract/specs/config/spec.md:5:A shared reader (`lib/config.js`) SHALL be the single entry for every process-config consumer: it scans line-by-line, treating fenced code blocks (``` open/close; an unterminated fence makes the rest of the file inert) and HTML comments (`<!-- … -->`, multi-line; unterminated likewise inert; first-open wins, no nesting) as non-content; a config row is any `|`-leading table row — first cell key, second cell value, extra columns ignored, header and separator rows skipped; duplicate rows with the same key and value are tolerated silently, while different values for one key are a CONFLICT problem. Config problems surface ONLY when the key is actually consumed. The `--no-cas` flag keeps explicit supremacy over any config state.

A cas CONFLICT or illegal value at consultation makes C7 BLOCKED naming the config error — bad config never equals a waiver, though the `--no-cas` flag still waives explicitly. A waiver is always visible in the gate output — never a silent skip. The waiver vocabulary is shared with `archive` (which denies by default since 4.0.1); verify's projection stays warn-only. In-flight only: at the archived stage the deltas are already merged and C7 reports n/a.

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * process-config parses as structure, never as full-text regex (config-contract).
     4	 * One shared reader for every consumer: fenced code blocks and HTML comments are
     5	 * non-content (unterminated blocks make the rest of the file inert — naturally
     6	 * fail-closed for waiver keys); a config row is any |-leading table row, first
     7	 * cell key, second cell value, extra columns ignored; same-key same-value
     8	 * duplicates are tolerated, different values are a CONFLICT problem. Problems
     9	 * surface only when the key is actually consumed.
    10	 */
    11	const fs = require('fs');
    12	const path = require('path');
    13	
    14	// parse the raw text → { values: Map<key,value>, conflicts: Set<key> }
    15	function parseConfig(text) {
    16	  const values = new Map();
    17	  const conflicts = new Set();
    18	  let fence = false, comment = false;
    19	  for (const raw of (text || '').split('\n')) {
    20	    const line = raw.endsWith('\r') ? raw.slice(0, -1) : raw;
    21	    if (comment) { if (line.includes('-->')) comment = false; continue; }
    22	    if (fence) { if (/^\s*```/.test(line)) fence = false; continue; }
    23	    if (/^\s*```/.test(line)) { fence = true; continue; }
    24	    if (line.includes('<!--')) { if (!line.includes('-->')) comment = true; continue; }
    25	    if (!line.trimStart().startsWith('|')) continue;
    26	    const cells = line.split('|').map((c) => c.trim());
    27	    // cells[0] is the empty prefix before the leading pipe
    28	    const key = cells[1], value = cells[2];
    29	    if (!key || value === undefined || value === '') continue;
    30	    if (key.toLowerCase() === 'key' || /^-+$/.test(key)) continue;      // header / separator
    31	    if (/^-+$/.test(value)) continue;
    32	    if (values.has(key) && values.get(key) !== value) { conflicts.add(key); continue; }
    33	    values.set(key, value);
    34	  }
    35	  return { values, conflicts };
    36	}
    37	
    38	function readConfig(cwd) {
    39	  const p = path.join(cwd || '.', 'apriori', 'process-config.md');
    40	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) return { values: new Map(), conflicts: new Set() };
    41	  return parseConfig(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
    42	}
    43	
    44	// one key → { value: string|null, problem: string|null } (problem only when consumed)
    45	function getConfig(cwd, key) {
    46	  const { values, conflicts } = readConfig(cwd);
    47	  if (conflicts.has(key)) return { value: null, problem: `process-config carries conflicting '${key}' rows — resolve the conflict (one live row per key)` };
    48	  return { value: values.has(key) ? values.get(key) : null, problem: null };
    49	}
    50	
    51	module.exports = { parseConfig, readConfig, getConfig };

 succeeded in 157ms:
     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * Shared change-resolution helpers — one home so gate, status, and archive-merge
     4	 * can all use them without a require cycle (this module requires no lib sibling).
     5	 */
     6	const fs = require('fs');
     7	const path = require('path');
     8	
     9	const CHANGE_NAME_RE = /^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;   // bare kebab-case — same rule as `apriori new`
    10	
    11	// realpath containment (own copy — importing archive-merge's would create a cycle)
    12	function containsReal(root, target) {
    13	  let realRoot;
    14	  try { realRoot = fs.realpathSync(root); } catch { return false; }
    15	  let real;
    16	  try { real = fs.realpathSync(target); } catch { return false; }
    17	  return real === realRoot || real.startsWith(realRoot + path.sep);
    18	}
    19	
    20	// stage resolution: in-flight first, else newest archived (exact stamp regex, lexicographic last).
    21	// NAME IS NOT VALIDATED HERE — callers gate on CHANGE_NAME_RE first (a raw name reaches the regex below).
    22	function resolveChange(cwd, name) {
    23	  const changesDir = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
    24	  const inflight = path.join(changesDir, name);
    25	  if (fs.existsSync(inflight)) {
    26	    if (!containsReal(changesDir, inflight)) return { error: `change path escapes ${changesDir}` };
    27	    return { stage: 'in-flight', dir: inflight };
    28	  }
    29	  const archRoot = path.join(changesDir, 'archive');
    30	  if (fs.existsSync(archRoot)) {
    31	    const re = new RegExp(`^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}T\\d{4}-${name}$`);
    32	    // candidates must be DIRECTORIES (following symlinks) — a stray file with a stamp name is ignored
    33	    const hits = fs.readdirSync(archRoot).filter((b) => {
    34	      if (!re.test(b)) return false;
    35	      try { return fs.statSync(path.join(archRoot, b)).isDirectory(); } catch { return false; }
    36	    }).sort();
    37	    if (hits.length) {
    38	      const dir = path.join(archRoot, hits[hits.length - 1]);
    39	      // containment against the ARCHIVE root — a symlink pointing elsewhere under changes/ is still an escape
    40	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, dir)) return { error: `archived change path escapes ${archRoot}` };
    41	      return { stage: 'archived', dir };
    42	    }
    43	  }
    44	  return { error: `change '${name}' found neither at ${inflight} nor under ${archRoot}` };
    45	}
    46	
    47	// process-config `| cas | optional |` row — the project-level twin of --no-cas (flag wins).
    48	// Structured read (config-contract): fenced/commented rows grant nothing; a conflict is a
    49	// consumption-time problem, never a waiver. Returns the leading value token or null;
    50	// configCasProblem() surfaces the conflict for consumers that must fail closed on it.
    51	function configCas(cwd) {
    52	  const { value, problem } = require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'cas');
    53	  if (problem || value === null) return null;
    54	  return value.trim().split(/\s+/)[0].toLowerCase();
    55	}
    56	function configCasProblem(cwd) {
    57	  return require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'cas').problem;
    58	}
    59	
    60	// pre-4.0 scattered roots — existence-level probes only (lstat, never followed or read through)
    61	const LEGACY_ROOTS = ['requirement', 'spike', 'apriori/review', 'apriori/design', 'apriori/explore'];
    62	function legacyRoots(root) {
    63	  const hits = [];
    64	  for (const rel of LEGACY_ROOTS) {
    65	    try { fs.lstatSync(path.join(root, ...rel.split('/'))); hits.push(rel + '/'); } catch { /* absent */ }
    66	  }
    67	  return hits;
    68	}
    69	
    70	// file-level read guard: a path the tool is about to read must be a REGULAR file
    71	// contained (realpath) within its bundle — returns null when safe, else the defect
    72	function fileReadDefect(bundleDir, p) {
    73	  let st;
    74	  try { st = fs.lstatSync(p); } catch { return `missing: ${p}`; }
    75	  if (st.isSymbolicLink()) return `symlink: ${p}`;
    76	  if (!st.isFile()) return `not a regular file: ${p}`;
    77	  if (!containsReal(bundleDir, p)) return `escapes the change dir: ${p}`;
    78	  return null;
    79	}
    80	
    81	module.exports = { CHANGE_NAME_RE, containsReal, resolveChange, configCas, configCasProblem, legacyRoots, fileReadDefect };

   200	function checkBinding(cwd, name, stage, testCmd) {
   201	  const run = stage === 'in-flight'
   202	    ? verify({ change: name, cwd, testCmd })
   203	    : verify({ specs: [path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs')], cwd, testCmd });

   205	  const v = run.verdict;
   206	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0;
   207	  if (clean) return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'pass', detail: `verify GREEN (${stage})` }, projection: run.projection };
   208	  const parts = [];
   209	  for (const [label, arr] of [['red', v.boundRed], ['unbound', v.unbound], ['orphan', v.orphan], ['unidentified', v.unidentified], ['unattributed-failures', v.unattributed || []], ['duplicate-IDs', run.duplicates]])

   215	// C7: unstamped mutation deltas are denied by default — a waiver is always visible (GT-16).
   216	// Archived stage: the deltas are already merged; there is nothing left to stamp-check.
   217	function checkCas(projection, stage, noCas, cwd) {
   218	  if (stage === 'archived') return { id: 'C7', status: 'n/a', detail: 'deltas already merged' };
   219	  if (noCas) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (--no-cas)' };
   220	  const um = (projection && projection.unstampedMutations) || [];
   221	  const problem = um.length ? configCasProblem(cwd) : null;   // consumption-time only
   222	  if (problem) return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked', detail: `${problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver` };
   223	  if (configCas(cwd) === 'optional') return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (process-config cas: optional)' };
   224	  if (!um.length) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'every mutation delta is stamped' };
   225	  return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked',
   226	    detail: `unstamped mutation delta(s): ${um.join(', ')} — run: apriori stamp <store-file> (or waive with --no-cas / a process-config cas: optional row)` };
   227	}

   229	// The whole evaluation. → { code, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors }
   230	function runGate(opts) {
   231	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
   232	  const res = { code: 0, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'PASS', blocked: 0, errors: [], change: opts.change || null };
   233	  if (!opts.change) return err(res, 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--cwd <dir>] [--json] [--no-cas]');
   234	  if (!CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(opts.change)) return err(res, `invalid change name '${opts.change}' — bare kebab-case only`);
   235	  const loc = resolveChange(cwd, opts.change);
   236	  if (loc.error) return err(res, loc.error);
   237	  res.stage = loc.stage;
   238	  const flowPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'flow-state.md');
   239	  if (!fs.existsSync(flowPath)) return err(res, `no readable flow-state.md at ${flowPath} — tier-aware checks are impossible`);
   240	  let state;
   241	  try { state = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(flowPath, 'utf8')); }
   242	  catch (e) { return err(res, `flow-state unreadable: ${e.message}`); }
   243	  let testCmd = opts.testCmd;
   244	  if (!testCmd) {
   245	    const c = configTestCmd(cwd);
   246	    if (c && c.error) return err(res, `verify: ${c.error}`);
   247	    testCmd = c;
   248	  }
   249	  if (!testCmd) return err(res, 'no test command: pass --test-cmd or add a test-cmd row to apriori/process-config.md');
   250	
   251	  const b = checkBinding(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, testCmd);
   252	  if (b.infra) { for (const e of b.infra) res.errors.push(`verify: ${e}`); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
   253	  res.checks.push(b.check);
   254	  const tier = TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier) ? state.tier : null;
   255	  res.checks.push(checkTasks(loc.dir, tier));
   256	  res.checks.push(checkFlowState(state, opts.change));
   257	  const rdDefect = reviewDirDefect(loc.dir);
   258	  if (rdDefect) {
   259	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   260	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   261	  } else {
   262	    res.checks.push(checkLedger(tier, loc.stage, loc.dir));
   263	    res.checks.push(checkEvidence(loc.dir));
   264	  }
   265	
   266	  res.checks.push(checkKb(cwd, loc.dir));
   267	  res.checks.push(checkCas(b.projection, loc.stage, !!opts.noCas, cwd));
   268	
   269	  res.blocked = res.checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'blocked').length;
   270	  res.result = res.blocked ? 'BLOCKED' : 'PASS';
   271	  res.code = res.blocked ? 1 : 0;
   272	  return res;
   273	}

   275	function toJson(res) {
   276	  return { change: res.change, stage: res.stage, checks: res.checks, result: res.result, blocked: res.blocked, errors: res.errors };
   277	}

   281	function cli(argv) {

   283	    flags: { '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag', '--no-cas': 'flag' },
   284	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ change: null, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'ERROR', blocked: 0, errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   285	    const a = { change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null, cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'], noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   286	    const res = runGate(a);
   287	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   288	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('gate: ' + e);
   289	    const mark = { pass: '✓', blocked: '✗', 'n/a': '–' };
   290	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.status === 'blocked' ? 'BLOCKED — ' : ''}${c.detail}`);
   291	    if (res.code !== 2) console.log(res.code === 0 ? `\nGATE: PASS — ${CAVEAT}` : `\nGATE: BLOCKED (${res.blocked} item(s))`);
   292	    return res.code;
   293	  });

   313	// Discover a change's delta files. → { files: [{abs, suffix}], errors: [] } — errors are
   314	// VALIDATION class (exit 2 on both surfaces): bad name, missing dir, escape, zero files.
   315	function discoverDeltas(changesDirAbs, name) {
   316	  const errors = [];
   317	  if (!name || !CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(name)) return { files: [], errors: [`invalid change name '${name}' — bare kebab-case only`] };
   318	  const changeDir = path.join(changesDirAbs, name);
   319	  if (!fs.existsSync(changeDir)) return { files: [], errors: [`change '${name}' not found under ${changesDirAbs}`] };
   320	  if (!containsReal(changesDirAbs, changeDir)) return { files: [], errors: [`change path escapes ${changesDirAbs}: ${changeDir}`] };
   321	  const specsDir = path.join(changeDir, 'specs');
   322	  // a symlinked specs/ pointing outside would smuggle its target in as the containment ROOT —
   323	  // require specs/ itself to really live inside the change dir before trusting it as a root
   324	  if (fs.existsSync(specsDir) && !containsReal(changeDir, specsDir))
   325	    return { files: [], errors: [`specs dir escapes the change dir (symlink?): ${specsDir}`] };
   326	  const all = mdFilesUnder(specsDir);
   327	  if (!all.length) return { files: [], errors: [`no delta spec files (*.md) found under ${specsDir}`] };
   328	  const files = [];
   329	  for (const abs of all) {
   330	    if (!containsReal(specsDir, abs)) { errors.push(`delta path escapes the change root: ${abs}`); continue; }
   331	    files.push({ abs, suffix: path.relative(specsDir, abs).split(path.sep).join('/') });
   332	  }
   333	  return { files, errors };
   334	}
   335	
   336	// Build the projection: apply every delta to its mapped store file IN MEMORY.
   337	// → { texts: Map<suffix, projectedText>, modules, perModule, conflicts, casMismatches,
   338	//     hygiene, validation } — texts covers ALL store files (pass-through + projected).
   339	function buildProjection(storeRoot, deltaFiles, change) {
   340	  const texts = new Map(), perModule = new Map(), modules = [];
   341	  const conflicts = [], casMismatches = [], hygiene = [], validation = [];
   342	  const unstampedMutations = [], notes = [], repaired = [];
   343	  for (const abs of mdFilesUnder(storeRoot))
   344	    texts.set(path.relative(storeRoot, abs).split(path.sep).join('/'), fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8'));
   345	  for (const { abs, suffix } of deltaFiles) {
   346	    modules.push(suffix);
   347	    const target = path.join(storeRoot, ...suffix.split('/'));
   348	    if (!containsReal(storeRoot, target)) { validation.push(`store target escapes ${storeRoot}: ${target}`); continue; }
   349	    const raw = fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8');
   350	    if (!raw.trim()) { hygiene.push(`empty delta file: ${abs}`); continue; }
   351	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(raw);
   352	    if (problems.length) { hygiene.push(...problems.map((p) => `${abs}: ${p}`)); continue; }
   353	    if (deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) { hygiene.push(`parsed 0 delta operations from ${abs} — it has content but no recognized operations`); continue; }
   354	    if (!stamp && mutationOpCount(delta) > 0) unstampedMutations.push(suffix);   // WARN grade (callers print)
   355	    const storeText = texts.get(suffix) || '';
   356	    if (stamp) {
   357	      const actual = texts.has(suffix) ? fingerprint(storeText) : 'new';
   358	      if (stamp !== actual) {
   359	        // rerun repair: a mismatch whose every op is already applied is the resumed-rerun
   360	        // signature — divergence with ANY real pending op still fails exactly as before
   361	        const probe = merge(storeText, delta, change);
   362	        const pending = probe.merged.length + probe.modified.length + probe.deprecated.length + probe.renamed.length;
   363	        if (pending === 0 && probe.conflicts.length === 0) {
   364	          notes.push(`${suffix}: stamp mismatch but the delta is already fully applied — rerun accepted`);
   365	          perModule.set(suffix, probe);
   366	          repaired.push(suffix);
   367	          continue;                                          // contributes no writes
   368	        }
   369	        casMismatches.push(`base mismatch for ${target}: delta expects ${stamp}, store is ${actual}`); continue;
   370	      }
   371	    }
   372	    const r = merge(storeText, delta, change);
   373	    perModule.set(suffix, r);
   374	    if (r.conflicts.length) { conflicts.push(...r.conflicts.map((c) => `${suffix}: ${c}`)); continue; }
   375	    texts.set(suffix, renderStore(storeText, r.store));
   376	  }
   377	  modules.sort();
   378	  return { texts, modules, perModule, conflicts, casMismatches, hygiene, validation, unstampedMutations, notes, repaired };
   379	}

   381	// ---- archive commit machinery ----------------------------------------------
   382	
   383	// colon-free date-time stamp YYYY-MM-DDThhmm (CLI's own clock; never an agent guess)
   384	function archiveStamp(d) {
   385	  const p = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
   386	  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}T${p(d.getHours())}${p(d.getMinutes())}`;
   387	}

   389	const CHANGE_NAME_RE = /^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;   // bare kebab-case — same rule as `apriori new`
   390	
   391	
   392	// Move an in-flight change dir (bare name) → changes/archive/<stamp>-<name>/
   393	// Fail-closed: source AND destination must resolve (realpath) strictly inside changesDir.
   394	function archiveChangeDir(changesDir, name, now, ops) {
   395	  const rename = (ops && ops.renameSync) || fs.renameSync;
   396	  if (!CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(name)) throw new Error(`invalid change name '${name}' — bare kebab-case only`);
   397	  const src = path.join(changesDir, name);
   398	  if (!fs.existsSync(src)) return null;
   399	  if (!containsReal(changesDir, src)) throw new Error(`change path escapes ${changesDir}`);
   400	  const dest = path.join(changesDir, 'archive', `${archiveStamp(now)}-${name}`);
   401	  if (!containsReal(changesDir, dest)) throw new Error(`archive destination escapes ${changesDir} (symlinked archive/?)`);
   402	  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dest), { recursive: true });
   403	  rename(src, dest);
   404	  return dest;
   405	}
   406	
   407	const CATEGORY_LABELS = [['merged (ADDED)', 'merged'], ['modified (MODIFIED)', 'modified'],
   408	  ['deprecated (REMOVED)', 'deprecated'], ['renamed (RENAMED)', 'renamed'], ['already merged (no-op)', 'unchanged']];
   409	
   410	// High-level archive: the whole change, four phases (preflight → stage → commit → move).
   411	// → { code, out: [], err: [] } — pure return; cli() prints. ops is the fault-injection seam.
   412	function archiveChange(o) {
   413	  const out = [], err = [];
   414	  const ops = o.ops || { writeFileSync: fs.writeFileSync.bind(fs), renameSync: fs.renameSync.bind(fs), rmSync: fs.rmSync.bind(fs) };
   415	  const cwd = o.cwd || process.cwd();
   416	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, o.changesDir || path.join('apriori', 'changes'));
   417	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   418	
   419	  // --- phase 1: preflight (no writes) ---
   420	  const d = discoverDeltas(changesDir, o.change);
   421	  if (d.errors.length) { err.push(...d.errors.map((e) => `archive: ${e}`)); return { code: 2, out, err }; }
   422	  const p = buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, o.change);
   423	  if (p.validation.length) { err.push(...p.validation.map((e) => `archive: ${e}`)); return { code: 2, out, err }; }
   424	  // per-module report for whatever merged cleanly (dry-run info comes first, then failures)
   425	  for (const suffix of p.modules) {
   426	    const r = p.perModule.get(suffix);
   427	    if (!r || r.conflicts.length) continue;
   428	    out.push(`--- ${suffix}`);
   429	    for (const [label, key] of CATEGORY_LABELS) if (r[key].length) out.push(`${label}: ${r[key].join(', ')}`);
   430	  }
   431	  const casDenials = [];
   432	  if (p.unstampedMutations.length) {
   433	    const w = casWaiver(cwd, o.noCas);
   434	    if (w.problem) {
   435	      casDenials.push(`${w.problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver (denied)`);
   436	    } else if (!w.waiver) {
   437	      for (const sfx of p.unstampedMutations)
   438	        casDenials.push(`${unstampedWarning(sfx)} — denied by default; waive with --no-cas or a | cas | optional | config row`);
   439	    } else {
   440	      for (const sfx of p.unstampedMutations) out.push(`warning: ${unstampedWarning(sfx)}`);
   441	      out.push(`note: cas waived by ${w.waiver}`);
   442	    }
   443	  }
   444	  for (const n of p.notes) out.push(`note: ${n}`);
   445	  // CAS denial joins the preflight failure set — every diagnosis stays visible in one report
   446	  const preflightFailures = [...casDenials, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches, ...p.conflicts];
   447	  if (preflightFailures.length) {
   448	    for (const f of preflightFailures) err.push(`archive: ${f}`);
   449	    out.push('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   450	    return { code: 1, out, err };
   451	  }
   452	  const repaired = new Set(p.repaired);                     // already applied — no writes for these
   453	  const jobs = d.files.filter(({ suffix }) => !repaired.has(suffix)).map(({ suffix }) => {
   454	    const store = path.join(storeRoot, ...suffix.split('/'));
   455	    return { suffix, store, tmp: store + '.tmp-archive', text: p.texts.get(suffix) };
   456	  }).sort((a, b) => (a.store < b.store ? -1 : 1));
   457	  if (o.write) {
   458	    for (const j of jobs) if (fs.existsSync(j.tmp)) {
   459	      err.push(`archive: temp file already exists: ${j.tmp} — another run in flight or a manual-recovery artifact; not touching it`);
   460	      out.push('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   461	      return { code: 1, out, err };
   462	    }
   463	    if (o.changesDirExplicit) {
   464	      // destination containment is checked before any write, not at move time
   465	      const probe = path.join(changesDir, 'archive', 'probe');
   466	      if (!containsReal(changesDir, probe)) {
   467	        err.push(`archive: archive destination escapes ${changesDir} (symlinked archive/?)`);
   468	        return { code: 2, out, err };
   469	      }
   470	    }
   471	  }
   472	  if (!o.write) {
   473	    out.push(`\nRESULT: MERGED (dry-run; ${jobs.length} module(s)) — pass --write to apply`);
   474	    return { code: 0, out, err };
   475	  }
   476	
   477	  // --- phase 2: stage (this run's temps only — preflight proved none pre-existed) ---
   478	  const staged = [];
   479	  try {
   480	    for (const j of jobs) {
   481	      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(j.store), { recursive: true });
   482	      ops.writeFileSync(j.tmp, j.text);
   483	      staged.push(j.tmp);
   484	    }
   485	  } catch (e) {
   486	    for (const t of staged) { try { ops.rmSync(t, { force: true }); } catch { /* best effort */ } }
   487	    err.push(`archive: staging failed (${e.message}) — stores untouched, this run's temp files removed`);
   488	    return { code: 1, out, err };
   489	  }
   490	
   491	  // --- phase 3: commit (per-file atomic renames, sorted order; no rollback on mid-commit failure) ---
   492	  const committed = [];
   493	  for (const j of jobs) {
   494	    try { ops.renameSync(j.tmp, j.store); committed.push(j); }
   495	    catch (e) {
   496	      err.push(`archive: COMMIT FAILED at ${j.suffix} (${e.message})`);
   497	      err.push(`  committed:     ${committed.map((c) => c.suffix).join(', ') || '(none)'}`);
   498	      const remaining = jobs.filter((x) => !committed.includes(x));
   499	      err.push(`  NOT committed: ${remaining.map((x) => x.suffix).join(', ')}`);
   500	      err.push(`  temp files remaining for manual completion: ${remaining.map((x) => x.tmp).join(', ')}`);
   501	      return { code: 1, out, err };
   502	    }
   503	  }
   504	
   505	  // --- phase 4: move (only with explicit --changes-dir) ---
   506	  let moved = null;
   507	  if (o.changesDirExplicit) {
   508	    try { moved = archiveChangeDir(changesDir, o.change, new Date(), ops); }
   509	    catch (e) { err.push(`archive: stores committed but the change-dir move failed: ${e.message} — rerun to complete`); return { code: 1, out, err }; }
   510	    if (moved === null) { err.push('archive: stores committed but the change dir vanished before the move'); return { code: 1, out, err }; }
   511	  }
   512	  out.push(`\nRESULT: MERGED — ${jobs.length} module store(s) rewritten${moved ? `; change archived → ${moved}` : ''}`);
   513	  return { code: 0, out, err };
   514	}

   516	// print the CAS stamp line for a store file's current content
   517	const STAMP_USAGE = 'usage: apriori stamp <store-file>';
   518	function stampCli(argv) {
   519	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'stamp', usage: STAMP_USAGE, positionals: 1, flags: {} }, (f, pos) => {
   520	    const p = pos[0];
   521	    if (!fs.existsSync(p)) { console.log('<!-- apriori-base: new -->'); return 0; }
   522	    if (fs.statSync(p).isDirectory()) { console.error(`stamp: ${p} is a directory, not a store file`); return 2; }
   523	    let text;
   524	    try { text = fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'); }
   525	    catch (e) { console.error(`stamp: cannot read ${p}: ${e.message}`); return 2; }
   526	    console.log(`<!-- apriori-base: ${fingerprint(text)} -->`);
   527	    return 0;
   528	  });
   529	}

   531	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>] [--no-cas]\n   or: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>] [--no-cas]';
   532	
   533	function cli(argv) {
   534	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'archive', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   535	    flags: { '--store': 'value', '--delta': 'value', '--change': 'value', '--write': 'flag', '--changes-dir': 'value', '--no-cas': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   536	    const a = { store: f['--store'] || null, delta: f['--delta'] || null, change: f['--change'] || null,
   537	      write: !!f['--write'], changesDir: f['--changes-dir'] || null, noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   538	  // high-level form: --change alone (no --store/--delta) drives the whole change
   539	    if (a.change && !a.store && !a.delta) {
   540	      const r = archiveChange({ cwd: process.cwd(), change: a.change, write: a.write,
   541	        changesDir: a.changesDir || undefined, changesDirExplicit: !!a.changesDir, noCas: a.noCas });
   542	      for (const l of r.out) console.log(l);
   543	      for (const l of r.err) console.error(l);
   544	      return r.code;
   545	    }
   546	    if (a.change && (a.store || a.delta) && !(a.store && a.delta)) {
   547	      console.error('usage: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (high-level form)\n   or: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (single-file form)\n(--change cannot be combined with only one of --store/--delta)');
   548	      return 2;
   549	    }
   550	    if (!a.store || !a.delta || !a.change) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   551	    if (!CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(a.change)) { console.error(`archive: invalid change name '${a.change}' — bare kebab-case only (path segments are not allowed)`); return 2; }
   552	    if (a.changesDir && !fs.existsSync(path.join(a.changesDir, a.change))) {
   553	      console.error(`archive: change '${a.change}' not found under ${a.changesDir} — nothing to move`); return 2;
   554	    }
   555	    // first archive in a repo: the store file may not exist yet — start from an empty store
   556	    const storeExists = fs.existsSync(a.store);
   557	    const storeText = storeExists ? fs.readFileSync(a.store, 'utf8') : '';
   558	    if (!storeExists) console.log(`store ${a.store} does not exist yet — will be created${a.write ? '' : ' on --write'}`);
   559	    const deltaText = fs.readFileSync(a.delta, 'utf8');
   560	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(deltaText);
   561	    if (problems.length) {
   562	      for (const p of problems) console.error(`archive: ${a.delta}: ${p}`);
   563	      console.log('\nRESULT: MALFORMED DELTA — nothing written');
   564	      return 1;
   565	    }
   566	    // Guard against a silent no-op: a delta with real content that parsed to zero operations
   567	    // is almost always a format mismatch (wrong heading level/keyword), not an empty change.
   568	    if (deltaText.trim() && deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) {
   569	      console.error(`archive: parsed 0 delta operations from ${a.delta} — it has content but no recognized operations.`);
   570	      console.error('  expected `## ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED Requirements` (h2) with `### Requirement: <name>` (h3) blocks,');
   571	      console.error('  or `## RENAMED Requirements` with `- Old -> New` lines. Nothing written.');
   572	      return 1;
   573	    }
   574	    // AM-32 (4.0): mutation ops without a stamp are denied unless visibly waived
   575	    if (!stamp && mutationOpCount(delta) > 0) {
   576	      const w = casWaiver(process.cwd(), a.noCas);
   577	      if (w.problem || !w.waiver) {
   578	        console.error(`archive: ${w.problem ? w.problem + ' — a broken config never equals a waiver (denied)' : unstampedWarning(a.delta) + ' — denied by default; waive with --no-cas or a | cas | optional | config row'}`);
   579	        console.log('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   580	        return 1;
   581	      }
   582	      console.log(`warning: ${unstampedWarning(a.delta)}`);
   583	      console.log(`note: cas waived by ${w.waiver}`);
   584	    }
   585	    // CAS: a present base stamp must match the store the delta is about to merge into (AM-24/AM-26)
   586	    if (stamp) {
   587	      const actual = storeExists ? fingerprint(storeText) : 'new';
   588	      if (stamp !== actual) {
   589	        console.error(`archive: base mismatch for ${a.store}: delta expects ${stamp}, store is ${actual} — nothing written (serialize per §4.11)`);
   590	        return 1;
   591	      }
   592	    }
   593	    const { store, merged, modified, deprecated, renamed, conflicts, unchanged } = merge(storeText, delta, a.change);
   594	
   595	    for (const [label, ids] of [['merged (ADDED)', merged], ['modified (MODIFIED)', modified], ['deprecated (REMOVED)', deprecated], ['renamed (RENAMED)', renamed], ['already merged (no-op)', unchanged]])
   596	      if (ids.length) console.log(`${label}: ${ids.join(', ')}`);
   597	
   598	    if (conflicts.length) {
   599	      console.log('\nCONFLICTS (stop — open a ledger issue, human resolves):');
   600	      for (const c of conflicts) console.log(`  ✗ ${c}`);
   601	      console.log('\nRESULT: CONFLICT — nothing written');
   602	      return 1;
   603	    }
   604	    if (a.write) {
   605	      // Transactional ordering: stage the store to a temp file, do the (atomic) dir move, then
   606	      // commit the store via rename. If the move fails, the temp is discarded and the store on
   607	      // disk stays byte-for-byte untouched — never a half-completed archive.
   608	      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(a.store), { recursive: true });
   609	      const tmp = a.store + '.tmp-archive';
   610	      fs.writeFileSync(tmp, renderStore(storeText, store));

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * spec-runner — bind spec scenario IDs to test runs, report per-scenario red/green.
     4	 * VISION endgame, weak form: "spec IS the test suite". Zero deps — pure Node stdlib.
     5	 * Coupling surface is tiny and language-agnostic: markdown scenarios + a TAP-emitting test command.
     6	 *
     7	 * FAIL-CLOSED: a deterministic gate must refuse to say GREEN when its inputs are missing,
     8	 * its instrument misbehaved, or its evidence is ambiguous. Vacuous success is a bug.
     9	 * Exit codes: 0 = GREEN · 1 = spec/test gaps · 2 = invocation or infrastructure error.

    11	const fs = require('fs');
    12	const path = require('path');
    13	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    14	// merge-family helpers shared with archive (3.1 projection); archive-merge never requires spec-runner
    15	const am = require('./archive-merge');

    18	const DEFAULT_ID = '[A-Z]+-\\d+';
    19	const SCENARIO_RE = /^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/gm;

    21	const TAP_RE = /^(ok|not ok)\s+\d+\s+-\s+(.*)$/gm;
    22	
    23	// Leading scenario/test ID. The match must end at a word boundary: `XX-01b` is NOT `XX-01` —
    24	// silently truncating would merge two different scenarios into one binding.
    25	function leadId(text, idRe) {
    26	  const m = text.trim().match(idRe);
    27	  if (!m || m.index !== 0) return null;
    28	  const next = text.trim()[m[0].length];
    29	  if (next !== undefined && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(next)) return null;
    30	  return m[0];
    31	}
    32	
    33	function stripFences(text) { return text.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, ''); }
    34	
    35	function mdFiles(target) {
    36	  if (!fs.existsSync(target)) return [];
    37	  const st = fs.statSync(target);
    38	  if (st.isFile()) return [target];
    39	  const out = [];
    40	  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(target, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    41	    const p = path.join(target, e.name);
    42	    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...mdFiles(p));
    43	    else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(p);
    44	  }
    45	  return out.sort();
    46	}
    47	
    48	// Shared scenario scan over one text. Fenced content is documentation; scenarios inside
    49	// DEPRECATED requirement blocks (the marker REMOVED injects) are excluded in every form —
    50	// they are neither demanded nor listed, and their lingering tests become ORPHAN.
    51	function scanText(text, label, idRe, acc) {
    52	  const clean = am.stripDeprecatedBlocks(stripFences(text));
    53	  let m;
    54	  SCENARIO_RE.lastIndex = 0;
    55	  while ((m = SCENARIO_RE.exec(clean)) !== null) {
    56	    const id = leadId(m[1], idRe);
    57	    if (id) {
    58	      if (!acc.byId.has(id)) { acc.byId.set(id, []); acc.idFiles.set(id, []); }
    59	      acc.byId.get(id).push(m[1]);
    60	      acc.idFiles.get(id).push(label);
    61	    } else acc.unidentified.push([label, m[1]]);
    62	  }
    63	}
    64	
    65	function dupsOf(acc) {
    66	  return [...acc.byId.entries()].filter(([, titles]) => titles.length > 1)
    67	    .map(([id]) => ({ id, files: [...new Set(acc.idFiles.get(id))] }));
    68	}
    69	
    70	// Collect scenarios from spec files.
    71	// → { byId, unidentified:[[file,title]], files, missingTargets:[], duplicates:[{id,files}] }
    72	function collectScenarios(specTargets, idRe) {
    73	  const acc = { byId: new Map(), idFiles: new Map(), unidentified: [] };
    74	  const missingTargets = specTargets.filter((t) => !fs.existsSync(t));
    75	  const files = [...new Set(specTargets.flatMap(mdFiles))].sort();
    76	  for (const f of files) scanText(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'), f, idRe, acc);
    77	  return { byId: acc.byId, unidentified: acc.unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates: dupsOf(acc) };
    78	}
    79	
    80	// Same collection over in-memory texts (the projection path) — label = store-relative suffix.
    81	function collectScenariosFromTexts(texts, idRe) {
    82	  const acc = { byId: new Map(), idFiles: new Map(), unidentified: [] };
    83	  for (const [label, text] of texts) scanText(text, label, idRe, acc);
    84	  return { byId: acc.byId, unidentified: acc.unidentified, files: [...texts.keys()], missingTargets: [], duplicates: dupsOf(acc) };
    85	}

    87	// ---- TAP lexer (tap-contract): TAP is a version-aware protocol, not a pile of regexes ----
    88	const PLAN_LINE_RE = /^1\.\.(\d+)\s*(?:#.*)?$/;
    89	const POINT_LINE_RE = /^(?:ok|not ok)(?:\s|$)/;
    90	const POINT_NUM_RE = /^(?:ok|not ok)\s+(\d+)(?:\s|$)/;   // complete numeric token only — `ok 1abc` stays unnumbered
    91	const SUPPORTED_TAP_VERSIONS = new Set([12, 13, 14]);
    92	
    93	// split at the first UNESCAPED '#' (backslash escapes the next char); decode \\ and \# in the
    94	// description ONLY (other backslash pairs stay literal) — so \# is never a directive delimiter
    95	function splitDirective(rest) {
    96	  let desc = '', directive = null;
    97	  for (let i = 0; i < rest.length; i++) {
    98	    const c = rest[i];
    99	    if (c === '\\' && i + 1 < rest.length) { desc += c + rest[i + 1]; i++; continue; }
   100	    if (c === '#') { directive = rest.slice(i + 1); break; }
   101	    desc += c;
   102	  }
   103	  const isSkipTodo = directive !== null && /^\s*(skip|todo)/i.test(directive);
   104	  const decoded = desc.replace(/\\([\\#])/g, '$1').trim();
   105	  return { desc: decoded, isSkipTodo };
   106	}
   107	
   108	// does a text carry TAP-shaped tokens? (misrouted-channel detection — bail-out included)
   109	function looksLikeTap(text) {
   110	  return /^(?:TAP version |(?:not )?ok\b|1\.\.\d)/m.test(text || '') || /^\s*bail out!/im.test(text || '');
   111	}
   112	
   113	// Lex normalized stdout into typed top-level tokens. Line endings normalize first
   114	// (CRLF and lone-CR both end a line — a failure never hides behind a line-ending style).
   115	function lexTap(stdout) {
   116	  const lines = stdout.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').split('\n');
   117	  const tokens = [], problems = [];
   118	  let yamlOpen = -1;
   119	  for (let idx = 0; idx < lines.length; idx++) {
   120	    const line = lines[idx], n = idx + 1;
   121	    if (yamlOpen >= 0) {                                   // inside a top-level YAML block: opaque
   122	      if (line === '...') yamlOpen = -1;
   123	      continue;
   124	    }
   125	    if (/^\s*bail out!/i.test(line) && line.trim()) {      // case-insensitive, indentation-blind
   126	      tokens.push({ kind: 'bailout', line: line.trim(), n }); continue;
   127	    }
   128	    if (/^\s/.test(line)) continue;                        // indented = nested subtest detail
   129	    if (line === '---') { yamlOpen = n; continue; }
   130	    if (line.startsWith('TAP version ')) {
   131	      const m = /^TAP version (\d+)$/.exec(line);
   132	      tokens.push({ kind: 'version', num: m ? Number(m[1]) : null, raw: line, n }); continue;
   133	    }
   134	    let m;
   135	    if ((m = PLAN_LINE_RE.exec(line))) { tokens.push({ kind: 'plan', total: Number(m[1]), n }); continue; }
   136	    if (/^pragma [+-]\w+$/.test(line)) continue;           // the ONLY ignored TAP-14 construct
   137	    if (POINT_LINE_RE.test(line)) {
   138	      const fail = line.startsWith('not ok');
   139	      const nm = POINT_NUM_RE.exec(line);
   140	      let rest = line.slice(fail ? 'not ok'.length : 'ok'.length);
   141	      if (nm) rest = rest.replace(/^\s+\d+/, '');
   142	      rest = rest.replace(/^\s+/, '');
   143	      const { desc, isSkipTodo } = splitDirective(rest);
   144	      const description = desc.replace(/^-\s+/, '');       // the dash separator is optional (TAP)
   145	      tokens.push({ kind: 'point', fail, num: nm ? Number(nm[1]) : null, description, isSkipTodo, line, n });
   146	      continue;
   147	    }
   148	    // anything else at column 0 is diagnostics (comments, prose) — including `ok:`-style prefixes
   149	  }
   150	  if (yamlOpen >= 0) problems.push(`unterminated YAML diagnostic block opened at line ${yamlOpen} — a stray --- must not swallow the rest of the stream`);

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori doctor — diagnose the project↔apriori seam. Seven checks, one exit code:
     4	 * 0 = HEALTHY · 1 = FINDINGS · 2 = UNUSABLE (Node floor, uninitialized, usage error).
     5	 * Doctor never repairs and never writes; its ONLY side effect is D5 running the
     6	 * project's test command once (--no-run removes even that). Findings name their fixer.
     7	 */
     8	const fs = require('fs');
     9	const path = require('path');
    10	const { TOOLS, detectTools } = require('./init');
    11	const { checkRunbookFreshness } = require('./check');
    12	const { collectScenarios, parseTap, zeroTapParsed, runTestCommand, configTestCmd, DEFAULT_ID, looksLikeTap } = require('./spec-runner');
    13	const { activeChanges, parseFlowState } = require('./status');
    14	const { containsReal } = require('./archive-merge');

    18	const NODE_FLOOR = 22;
    19	
    20	// type-honest probes: doctor's whole job is malformed scaffolds — a file impersonating a
    21	// directory (or vice versa) must become a FINDING, never a false pass or a crash
    22	function isDir(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isDirectory(); } catch { return false; } }
    23	function isFile(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isFile(); } catch { return false; } }
    24	function readIfFile(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isFile() ? fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8') : null; } catch { return null; } }
    25	
    26	// D5's classifier over a runTestCommand result — exported for the Windows-safe signal test (DR-06)
    27	function classifyProbe(run) {
    28	  // run = runTestCommand's shape: { out, status, signal, error }
    29	  const { out } = run;
    30	  if (run.error) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command failed to spawn: ${run.error}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    31	  if (run.signal) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command was killed by signal ${run.signal}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    32	  const idRe = new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID);
    33	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, tapProblems } = parseTap(out, idRe);
    34	  if (bailout) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test run aborted: ${bailout}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    35	  // the lexer's protocol problems (unsupported version, YAML, plan placement) are seam findings (DR-14)
    36	  if (tapProblems && tapProblems.length)
    37	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `TAP protocol: ${tapProblems[0]}`, fix: 'emit supported TAP (12/13/14 or no version line) from the test command' };
    38	  // TAP on stderr with a silent stdout: the channel is misrouted, not missing (DR-14)
    39	  if (!out.trim() && looksLikeTap(run.stderr))
    40	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'TAP arrives on stderr while stdout is silent — verify reads stdout only', fix: 'append 2>&1 to the test command' };
    41	  if (!out.trim()) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'test command produced no output', fix: 'fix the test command' };
    42	  if (zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures))
    43	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'output is not TAP — node test runner needs --test-reporter=tap', fix: 'emit TAP from the test command' };
    44	  const parsed = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0) + untagged.length + unattributedFailures.length;
    45	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
    46	  // an unexplained non-zero exit is classified BEFORE any ok branch — a non-zero `1..0` run lands here
    47	  if (run.status !== null && run.status !== 0 && failCount === 0)
    48	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command exited ${run.status} unexplained by TAP — truncated?`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    49	  if (parsed === 0) {
    50	    if (/^1\.\.0\b/m.test(out)) return { id: 'D5', status: 'ok', detail: 'TAP plumbing OK (empty suite, 1..0)' };
    51	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'TAP stream truncated or malformed (version/plan but zero result lines)', fix: 'fix the test command' };
    52	  }
    53	  const pass = parsed - failCount;
    54	  return { id: 'D5', status: 'ok', detail: `TAP plumbing OK (${pass}p/${failCount}f — test failures are verify's business, not doctor's)` };
    55	}
    56	
    57	// The whole diagnosis. → { code, result, findings, checks, errors }
    58	function runDoctor(opts = {}) {
    59	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
    60	  const checks = [], errors = [];
    61	  const finish = (code) => {
    62	    const findings = checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'finding').length;
    63	    const result = code === 2 ? 'UNUSABLE' : (findings ? 'FINDINGS' : 'HEALTHY');
    64	    return { code: code !== undefined ? code : (findings ? 1 : 0), result, findings, checks, errors };
    65	  };
    66	
    67	  // D1 — Node floor (version injectable for tests, DR-12)
    68	  const major = parseInt((opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node), 10);
    69	  if (major < NODE_FLOOR) {
    70	    checks.push({ id: 'D1', status: 'finding', detail: `Node ${opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node} is below the supported floor (>= ${NODE_FLOOR})`, fix: 'upgrade Node' });
    71	    errors.push(`Node below the supported floor (>= ${NODE_FLOOR})`);
    72	    return finish(2);
    73	  }
    74	  checks.push({ id: 'D1', status: 'ok', detail: `Node ${opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node} >= ${NODE_FLOOR}` });
    75	
    76	  // D2 — init scaffold
    77	  const ap = path.join(cwd, 'apriori');
    78	  if (!isDir(ap)) {
    79	    checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'finding', detail: `no apriori/ here — not an apriori project yet`, fix: `run 'apriori init'` });
    80	    errors.push(`no apriori/ directory — run 'apriori init'`);
    81	    return finish(2);
    82	  }
    83	  const gaps = [];
    84	  if (!isFile(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md'))) gaps.push([`apriori/runbook.md ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md')) ? 'is not a regular file' : 'missing'}`, `apriori init`]);
    85	  if (!isDir(path.join(ap, 'specs'))) gaps.push([`apriori/specs/ ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'specs')) ? 'is not a directory' : 'missing (half-initialized scaffold)'}`, `apriori init`]);
    86	  const giText = readIfFile(path.join(ap, '.gitignore'));
    87	  if (giText === null || !/^tmp\/$/m.test(giText)) gaps.push(['apriori/.gitignore missing, unreadable, or missing its tmp/ line', `apriori update`]);
    88	  if (!isDir(path.join(ap, 'tmp'))) gaps.push([`apriori/tmp/ ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'tmp')) ? 'is not a directory' : 'dir missing'}`, `apriori update / mkdir`]);
    89	  if (gaps.length) for (const [detail, fix] of gaps) checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'finding', detail, fix });
    90	  else checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'ok', detail: 'scaffold complete' + (fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'process-config.md')) ? '' : ' (no process-config.md — optional, defaults apply)') });
    91	
    92	  // D3 — runbook freshness (absence OR wrong type is D2's finding; D3 must never contradict or crash)
    93	  if (!isFile(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md')))
    94	    checks.push({ id: 'D3', status: 'n/a', detail: 'runbook missing or not a regular file — see D2; freshness not checkable' });
    95	  else {
    96	    const warn = checkRunbookFreshness(cwd);
    97	    checks.push(warn.length
    98	      ? { id: 'D3', status: 'finding', detail: warn[0], fix: 'apriori update' }
    99	      : { id: 'D3', status: 'ok', detail: 'runbook copy matches the installed CLI' });
   100	  }
   101	
   102	  // D4 — tool pointers for DETECTED tools only
   103	  const detected = detectTools(cwd);
   104	  if (!detected.length) checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no known AI-tool markers detected' });
   105	  else {
   106	    let broken = 0;
   107	    for (const key of detected) {
   108	      const t = TOOLS[key];
   109	      const rules = path.join(cwd, t.rules);
   110	      const rulesText = readIfFile(rules);
   111	      if (rulesText === null || !rulesText.includes('apriori/runbook.md')) {
   112	        checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'finding', detail: `${t.name}: ${t.rules} ${rulesText !== null ? 'lost its runbook pointer' : (fs.existsSync(rules) ? 'is not a regular readable file' : 'is missing')}`, fix: 'apriori init' });
   113	        broken++;
   114	      }
   115	      if (t.command && !isFile(path.join(cwd, t.command))) {
   116	        checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'finding', detail: `${t.name}: command file ${t.command} is missing`, fix: 'apriori init' });
   117	        broken++;
   118	      }
   119	    }
   120	    if (!broken) checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'ok', detail: `pointers intact for: ${detected.join(', ')}` });
   121	  }
   122	
   123	  // D5 — TAP probe (the one declared side effect; --no-run skips)
   124	  const cfgCmd = opts.testCmd ? null : configTestCmd(cwd);
   125	  const testCmd = opts.testCmd || (cfgCmd && cfgCmd.error ? null : cfgCmd);
   126	  if (cfgCmd && cfgCmd.error) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `config: ${cfgCmd.error}`, fix: 'keep one live test-cmd row' });
   127	  else if (!testCmd) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no test command configured — pass --test-cmd or set one via apriori init --test-cmd' });
   128	  else if (opts.noRun) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'probe skipped (--no-run)' });
   129	  else checks.push(classifyProbe(runTestCommand(testCmd, cwd)));
   130	
   131	  // D6 — store health (no test run; default ID pattern only)
   132	  const specsDir = path.join(ap, 'specs');
   133	  if (!fs.existsSync(specsDir)) checks.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no store dir — see D2' });
   134	  else {
   135	    const { byId, unidentified, files, duplicates } = collectScenarios([specsDir], new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID));
   136	    if (!files.length || (byId.size === 0 && unidentified.length === 0))
   137	      checks.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'empty store — normal for a new project' });
   138	    else {
   139	      let bad = 0;
   140	      if (unidentified.length) { checks.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `scenario(s) without a bindable ${DEFAULT_ID} ID: ${unidentified.map(([, t]) => t.slice(0, 40)).join(' · ')}`, fix: 'add leading IDs' }); bad++; }
   141	      if (duplicates.length) { checks.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `duplicate scenario IDs: ${duplicates.map((d) => d.id).join(', ')}`, fix: 'deduplicate IDs' }); bad++; }
   142	      if (!bad) checks.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'ok', detail: `${byId.size} scenario(s), all bindable, no duplicates` });
   143	    }
   144	  }
   145	
   146	  // D7 — changes overview
   147	  const actives = activeChanges(cwd);
   148	  const notes = [];
   149	  let d7bad = 0;
   150	  const changesRoot = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   151	  for (const c of actives) {
   152	    const fp = path.join(changesRoot, c, 'flow-state.md');
   153	    if (!fs.existsSync(fp)) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: no flow-state.md`, fix: 'apriori new / restore the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   154	    // per-file containment: a symlinked flow-state pointing outside the changes tree is
   155	    // diagnosed, never read (doctor must not leak outside content into its report)
   156	    if (!containsReal(changesRoot, fp)) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state.md escapes apriori/changes/ (symlink?) — not read`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   157	    let st;
   158	    try { st = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8')); }
   159	    catch (e) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state unreadable (${e.message})`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   160	    if (!st.change) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state has no 'change:' key`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; }
   161	    else if (st.change !== c) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state says 'change: ${st.change}' — mismatches the dir`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; }
   162	    else notes.push(`${c} @ ${st['current-step'] || '?'}`);
   163	  }
   164	  const archRoot = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes', 'archive');
   165	  if (fs.existsSync(archRoot)) {
   166	    for (const b of fs.readdirSync(archRoot).sort()) {
   167	      if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T\d{4}-/.test(b)) continue;
   168	      const dir = path.join(archRoot, b);
   169	      let isDir = false;
   170	      try { isDir = fs.statSync(dir).isDirectory(); } catch { continue; }
   171	      if (!isDir) continue;
   172	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, dir)) { notes.push(`${b}: skipped (escapes archive/ — symlink?)`); continue; }
   173	      const fp = path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md');
   174	      if (!fs.existsSync(fp)) continue;
   175	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, fp)) { notes.push(`${b}: flow-state escapes archive/ (symlink?) — not read`); continue; }
   176	      const st = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8'));
   177	      const step = st['current-step'];
   178	      if (step && step !== 'DONE' && step !== 'ABANDONED') notes.push(`archived ${b} @ ${step} — gate ④ possibly pending`);
   179	    }
   180	  }

   116	  const dest = archiveChangeDir(changes, 'add-playback', now);

   118	  assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(dest, 'flow-state.md')));

   124	test('AM-08 a content-bearing delta that parses to zero operations is a hard error', () => {
   125	  // pure helper: opcount of an empty vs real delta
   126	  assert.strictEqual(deltaOpCount(parseDelta('nothing here\n')), 0);
   127	  assert.ok(deltaOpCount(parseDelta('## ADDED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: X\ntext\n')) > 0);
   128	  // cli: wrong heading level (h3 section instead of h2) → parses to 0 ops → error, nothing written, exit 1
   129	  const store = tmpFile('# spec\n\n### Requirement: Old\nbody\n');
   130	  const before = fs.readFileSync(store, 'utf8');
   131	  const badDelta = tmpFile('### ADDED Requirements\n\n#### Requirement: New\nstuff\n');   // wrong levels
   132	  const errs = [], outs = [];
   133	  const origErr = console.error, origLog = console.log;
   134	  console.error = (...a) => errs.push(a.join(' '));
   135	  console.log = (...a) => outs.push(a.join(' '));
   136	  let code;
   137	  try { code = cli(['--store', store, '--delta', badDelta, '--change', 'c', '--write']); }
   138	  finally { console.error = origErr; console.log = origLog; }
   139	  assert.strictEqual(code, 1);
   140	  const err = errs.join('\n');
   141	  assert.match(err, /parsed 0 delta operations/);
   142	  assert.match(err, /## ADDED\|MODIFIED\|REMOVED Requirements/);
   143	  assert.match(err, /### Requirement:/);
   144	  assert.match(err, /RENAMED/);
   145	  assert.doesNotMatch(outs.join('\n'), /RESULT: MERGED/);        // never claims a merge happened
   146	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(store, 'utf8'), before);    // nothing written
   147	  // a genuinely empty (whitespace-only) delta is NOT flagged by this guard — it reaches the
   148	  // normal path: clean dry-run, exit 0, and no "0 delta operations" error
   149	  const emptyDelta = tmpFile('   \n\n');
   150	  const e2 = [], o2 = [];
   151	  console.error = (...a) => e2.push(a.join(' ')); console.log = (...a) => o2.push(a.join(' '));
   152	  let code2;
   153	  try { code2 = cli(['--store', store, '--delta', emptyDelta, '--change', 'c']); }
   154	  finally { console.error = origErr; console.log = origLog; }
   155	  assert.strictEqual(code2, 0);
   156	  assert.doesNotMatch(e2.join('\n'), /0 delta operations/);
   157	  assert.match(o2.join('\n'), /RESULT: MERGED \(dry-run/);
   158	});

   401	
   402	// Fallback test command from apriori/process-config.md's `test-cmd` row (written by init --test-cmd)
   403	function configTestCmd(cwd) {
   404	  const { value, problem } = require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'test-cmd');
   405	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   406	  return value;
   407	}
   408	
   409	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori verify --specs <dir...> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n   or: apriori verify --change <name> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n(--test-cmd may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has a test-cmd row)';
   410	
   411	function cli(argv) {
   412	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'verify', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   413	    flags: { '--specs': 'multi', '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   414	    const a = { specs: f['--specs'] || [], change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null,
   415	      idPattern: f['--id-pattern'] || DEFAULT_ID, cwd: f['--cwd'] || '.', json: !!f['--json'] };
   416	    if (a.change && a.specs.length) {
   417	      console.error('verify: --specs cannot be combined with --change — the projection defines the spec set');
   418	      return 2;
   419	    }
   420	    if (!a.testCmd) {
   421	      const c = configTestCmd(a.cwd);                    // init --test-cmd persists it there
   422	      if (c && c.error) { console.error(`verify: ${c.error}`); return 2; }
   423	      a.testCmd = c;
   424	    }
   425	    if ((!a.specs.length && !a.change) || !a.testCmd) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   426	    const run = verify(a);
   427	    if (run.projection)
   428	      for (const sfx of run.projection.unstampedMutations || []) console.error('warning: ' + am.unstampedWarning(sfx));
   429	    if (run.errors.length && !a.json)
   430	      for (const e of run.errors) console.error('error: ' + e);
   431	    console.log(a.json ? JSON.stringify(verifyJson(run), null, 2)
   432	                       : formatReport(run.verdict, run.results, run.fileCount, { duplicates: run.duplicates, errors: run.errors, stderr: run.stderr }));
   433	    if (run.errors.length) return 2;
   434	    return run.verdict.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   435	  });
   436	}

   439	  zeroTapParsed, runTestCommand, evaluate, infraErrors, formatReport, verifyJson, verify,
   440	  configTestCmd, cli, DEFAULT_ID };

     3	<!-- Lives at `apriori/process-config.md` (scaffolded there by `apriori init`).
     4	     Governance: this file is owned by a human; the agent READS it and never writes it (RUNBOOK §1 R3).
     5	     Missing file → the defaults printed in RUNBOOK §4 apply.
     6	     Invalid value (< 1, or unparsable) → the default applies, with a warning.
     7	     Every review-stage cap has a hard floor of 1 — no review stage ever goes to zero. -->
     8	
     9	| Field | Value | Legal range | Default |
    10	|---|---|---|---|
    11	| language | auto | auto (match the human) / any language name, e.g. `中文`, `English` | auto |
    12	| cas | required | required = archive denies unstamped mutation deltas / optional = warn only (waiver visible) | required |
    13	| step0-cap | 5 | ≥ 1 | 5 |
    14	| step2-cap | 4 | ≥ 1 | 4 |
    15	| step5-cap | 25 | ≥ 1 | 25 |
    16	| step6-cap | 4 | ≥ 1 | 4 |
    17	| spike-cap | 10 | ≥ 1 | 10 |
    18	| extraction-review-cap | 2 | ≥ 1 | 2 |
    19	| shrink-state | none | none / <stage>: <new-cap> entries, human-approved only | none |
    20	| rejected-ratio-guard | 50% | 0–100%; above it, shrinking is blocked | 50% |
    21	| shrink-proposal-freq | 5 | ≥ 1 — a proposal every N changes | 5 |
    22	| post-merge-review-freq | 1 in 5 | ≥ 1 in N merged changes (sampling rate) | 1 in 5 |

     1	<!-- apriori-base: new -->
     2	## ADDED Requirements
     3	
     4	### Requirement: process-config parses as structure, never as full-text regex
     5	A shared reader (`lib/config.js`) SHALL be the single entry for every process-config consumer: it scans line-by-line, treating fenced code blocks (``` open/close; an unterminated fence makes the rest of the file inert) and HTML comments (`<!-- … -->`, multi-line; unterminated likewise inert; first-open wins, no nesting) as non-content; a config row is any `|`-leading table row — first cell key, second cell value, extra columns ignored, header and separator rows skipped; duplicate rows with the same key and value are tolerated silently, while different values for one key are a CONFLICT problem. Config problems surface ONLY when the key is actually consumed. The `--no-cas` flag keeps explicit supremacy over any config state.
     6	
     7	#### Scenario: CF-01 fenced and commented rows never take effect
     8	- WHEN process-config carries `| cas | optional |` inside a fenced block (or an HTML comment) and `| cas | required |` as a live row, with an unstamped MODIFIED delta being archived
     9	- THEN the archive is DENIED (the live `required` wins; the fenced example grants nothing) — and the reversed layout (fenced `required`, live `optional`) waives with the waiver named
    10	
    11	#### Scenario: CF-02 multi-column rows parse by their first two cells
    12	- WHEN a live row reads `| cas | optional | 注释说明 |` (template-style extra column)
    13	- THEN the key/value parse as cas/optional and the extra cell is ignored
    14	
    15	#### Scenario: CF-03 duplicates tolerate sameness and refuse conflict
    16	- WHEN one config carries `| cas | optional |` twice, and another carries `| cas | optional |` plus `| cas | required |`
    17	- THEN the first reads cleanly as optional; the second is a CONFLICT — at consumption the archive errors (exit 1, nothing written) and gate C7 blocks naming the config conflict, never treating it as a waiver
    18	
    19	#### Scenario: CF-04 unterminated blocks are inert, not effective
    20	- WHEN a fence opens and never closes before rows that would otherwise waive CAS (same for an unterminated HTML comment)
    21	- THEN those rows grant nothing — the file's tail is inert and an unstamped mutation archive is denied
    22	
    23	#### Scenario: CF-05 config errors surface only at consumption
    24	- WHEN the config carries a cas CONFLICT but the archived change is fully stamped (or ADDED-only)
    25	- THEN the archive proceeds normally — the cas key was never consulted, so the bad row is invisible to this run
    26	
    27	#### Scenario: CF-06 the waiver is discoverable
    28	- WHEN `apriori archive` and `apriori gate` print their usage
    29	- THEN both list `--no-cas`
    30	
    31	#### Scenario: CF-07 the template names the cas row
    32	- WHEN `templates/process-config.md` is read
    33	- THEN it carries a `cas` row example documenting required-vs-optional semantics

     2	## ADDED Requirements
     3	
     4	### Requirement: archive consults CAS config through the structured reader
     5	`archive`'s waiver lookup SHALL go through the shared structured config reader: fenced/commented rows grant nothing, a live row decides, and a cas CONFLICT or illegal value at consultation is a preflight error (exit 1, nothing written or moved) naming the config problem — bad config never equals a waiver; `--no-cas` keeps explicit supremacy; stamped or ADDED-only runs never consult the key, so a bad row cannot affect them.
     6	
     7	#### Scenario: AM-42 the reviewer's fenced-waiver bypass is dead
     8	- WHEN an unstamped MODIFIED delta archives under a config whose fenced block says `| cas | optional |` while the live row says `| cas | required |` (the reviewer's reproduced bypass), and again under a cas CONFLICT, and again fully stamped under the same broken configs
     9	- THEN the first two runs are DENIED at preflight (nothing written; the conflict named in the second) while the stamped run proceeds untouched — and `--no-cas` still waives the first two explicitly
     1	<!-- apriori-base: sha256:c80793e6ddaa0be8610a0bb6b0c0a46328862ddf87dee76f7771ade135458bc0 -->
     2	## MODIFIED Requirements
     3	
     4	### Requirement: C7 denies unstamped mutation deltas unless visibly waived
     5	Gate SHALL run a seventh check: the change's projection carrying `unstampedMutations` → `C7 BLOCKED` naming each suffix and the stamp cure. Two escapes, flag over config: `gate --no-cas` → the check reports `waived (--no-cas)`; a process-config `cas` row read through the shared structured reader with value `optional` (leading token, case-insensitive; an absent row or `required` means required) → `waived (process-config)`. A cas CONFLICT or illegal value at consultation makes C7 BLOCKED naming the config error — bad config never equals a waiver, though the `--no-cas` flag still waives explicitly. A waiver is always visible in the gate output — never a silent skip. The waiver vocabulary is shared with `archive` (which denies by default since 4.0.1); verify's projection stays warn-only. In-flight only: at the archived stage the deltas are already merged and C7 reports n/a.
     6	
     7	#### Scenario: GT-16 C7 blocks, and waivers are loud
     8	- WHEN gate runs on a change whose delta carries unstamped mutation ops
     9	- THEN C7 reports BLOCKED naming the suffix and the cure; with --no-cas or the live `| cas | optional |` config row it reports the waiver by name instead of blocking (the flag also wins when the config says required), and a stamped or ADDED-only change passes C7 silently
    10	
    11	#### Scenario: GT-17 bad cas config blocks instead of waiving
    12	- WHEN the process-config carries a cas CONFLICT (two live rows, different values) or a fenced-only `optional` row, and gate runs on an unstamped-mutation change
    13	- THEN C7 reports BLOCKED — naming the config conflict in the first case, and the missing waiver in the second (fenced rows grant nothing); adding `--no-cas` waives either way

     7	#### Scenario: SR-49 a conflicted test-cmd refuses to run

     9	- THEN the conflicted run is RESULT: ERROR (exit 2) naming the config conflict, and the fenced-only run behaves as if no test-cmd were configured (usage error) — never executing the fenced example

   186	// mutation ops can silently clobber concurrent edits; ADDED-only deltas conflict or no-op instead
   187	function mutationOpCount(delta) {
   188	  return delta.MODIFIED.size + delta.REMOVED.size + delta.RENAMED.length;
   189	}
   190	
   191	// the one warning text every surface shares (WARN grade this minor; 4.0 makes stamps mandatory)
   192	// 4.0: unstamped mutation deltas are DENIED by default; --no-cas or the config row waives visibly
   193	// waiver source, or {problem} when the config itself is broken (deny + name it) — the
   194	// explicit flag stays supreme even over a broken config (the flag is a human's will)
   195	function casWaiver(cwd, noCas) {
   196	  if (noCas) return { waiver: '--no-cas' };
   197	  const problem = configCasProblem(cwd);
   198	  if (problem) return { problem };
   199	  if (configCas(cwd) === 'optional') return { waiver: 'process-config cas: optional' };
   200	  return {};
   201	}

     1	# 需求:config-contract —— process-config 是配置,不是全文 regex (final = v2)
     2	
     3	> change: `config-contract` · tier: medium · track: harden
     4	> lineage: v4;不合并 main/v1/v3
     5	> 来源:GPT-5.6 四审 P0-2 + P2-4,豁免绕过已本机复现。
     6	> v2 修订:CC-1..4(坏配置消费面矩阵、多列表行、fence/注释边界、消费时点)。
     7	
     8	## 目标
     9	
    10	`process-config.md` 的读取目前是对整份 Markdown 做首匹配 regex(`configCas`、`configTestCmd` 同病)。fenced 代码块、HTML 注释里的示例行会被当成生效配置;重复/冲突行"第一行获胜"且无声。本变更引入**一个共享的结构化配置读取器**,所有配置消费方走同一入口。
    11	
    12	## 行为需求
    13	
    14	1. **共享解析器 `readConfig(cwd)`**(建议落在 lib/resolve.js 或独立小模块):
    15	   - 逐行扫描,**跳过 fenced 代码块**(``` 开闭,与 spec-runner 的 fence 语义一致)与 **HTML 注释**(`<!-- … -->`,含多行);
    16	   - 只识别**有效配置行**:以 `|` 开头的表行,**第一格为 key、第二格为 value,多余列(注释列等)忽略**(CC-2,兼容现有模板);表头/分隔行按现状忽略;
    17	   - 产出 `{values: Map<key,value>, problems: string[]}`:**同 key 异值 → problem(冲突)**;**同 key 同值重复 → 静默容忍**(CC-3 决策);
    18	   - **边界语义(CC-3)**:未闭合 fence → 其后至 EOF 全部视为 fence 内(行不生效——对豁免类 key 天然 fail-closed);未闭合 HTML 注释同理;fence 与注释不嵌套解析(先到先占);
    19	   - 解析器纯函数、可单测。
    20	2. **消费方切换 + fail-closed 矩阵(CC-1/CC-4)**:
    21	   - **配置错误只在"该 key 被实际消费"时生效**(CC-4):stamped/ADDED-only 的 archive 不咨询 `cas`,坏 `cas` 行不影响它;一旦消费——
    22	   - `configCas`:`cas` 冲突或值非 `optional`/`required` → 消费面报配置错误:archive **exit 1、不写任何东西**;gate C7 **blocked** 点名配置错误——坏配置绝不等价于豁免;
    23	   - `configTestCmd`:`test-cmd` 冲突 → verify **infra ERROR exit 2**(点名配置冲突,绝不静默选一行跑);缺失照旧走 usage 路径;gate 继承;doctor 的 D5 探针在同况下 **finding** 点名配置错误;
    24	   - `language` 等其他消费方(全库 grep 为准)统一走 readConfig;它们的坏值按各自消费面现有降级语义(见 STEP2 枚举),但绝不读到 fence/注释内容;
    25	   - `--no-cas` flag 保持显式最高优先级(flag 在,配置坏也可明确豁免——flag 是人的显式意志)。
    26	3. **可发现性(P2-4)**:
    27	   - `archive`/`gate` 的 usage 文本列出 `--no-cas`;
    28	   - `templates/process-config.md` 增加带注释的 `cas` 行示例(默认 required 语义,注明 optional 的含义);
    29	   - init 出的新项目因此天然可发现。
    30	4. **回归钉子**:复现输入(fenced optional + 生效 required)必须:archive 拒绝、gate C7 拒绝;反向排列(fenced required + 生效 optional)必须豁免生效;HTML 注释内的行无效;异值重复 → 消费面配置错误。
    31	
    32	## 非目标
    33	
    34	- YAML/TOML 等新配置格式;process-config.md 的表格形状不变。
    35	- 配置项白名单校验(未知 key 忽略如旧——只有已消费 key 的值域才校验)。
    36	
    37	## 约束
    38	
    39	- 零依赖;既有测试不回归;gate/archive/verify 的既有 C7/警告语义除"读取更准"外不变。
    40	
    41	## 开放问题
    42	
    43	- 无。

apriori/changes/config-contract/specs/config/spec.md:5:A shared reader (`lib/config.js`) SHALL be the single entry for every process-config consumer: it scans line-by-line, treating fenced code blocks (``` open/close; an unterminated fence makes the rest of the file inert) and HTML comments (`<!-- … -->`, multi-line; unterminated likewise inert; first-open wins, no nesting) as non-content; a config row is any `|`-leading table row — first cell key, second cell value, extra columns ignored, header and separator rows skipped; duplicate rows with the same key and value are tolerated silently, while different values for one key are a CONFLICT problem. Config problems surface ONLY when the key is actually consumed. The `--no-cas` flag keeps explicit supremacy over any config state.

codex
还有一个边界不一致：`| cas | optional | <!-- note -->` 这类 live row + 行内注释会被整行跳过。由于 spec 同时说“任意 `|` 开头表行取前两格、extra columns ignored”和“HTML comments are non-content”，这看起来也是实现低于 spec，而不只是测试缺口。

apriori/changes/config-contract/specs/config/spec.md:5:A shared reader (`lib/config.js`) SHALL be the single entry for every process-config consumer: it scans line-by-line, treating fenced code blocks (``` open/close; an unterminated fence makes the rest of the file inert) and HTML comments (`<!-- … -->`, multi-line; unterminated likewise inert; first-open wins, no nesting) as non-content; a config row is any `|`-leading table row — first cell key, second cell value, extra columns ignored, header and separator rows skipped; duplicate rows with the same key and value are tolerated silently, while different values for one key are a CONFLICT problem. Config problems surface ONLY when the key is actually consumed. The `--no-cas` flag keeps explicit supremacy over any config state.

codex
**Issues**

CCIMPL-1 — `cas` illegal value is not a config error.  
Spec/req 要求 `cas` 冲突或值非 `optional|required` 在 archive/gate 消费时 fail-closed 并点名 config error；但 [lib/config.js](/mnt/d/Workbench/misc/apriori-spec-development/lib/config.js:45) 只把 duplicate conflict 暴露为 problem，[lib/resolve.js](/mnt/d/Workbench/misc/apriori-spec-development/lib/resolve.js:51) 会把 `| cas | banana |` 当作普通 token 返回，archive/gate 最终只是“无 waiver”而不是“坏配置”。  
Fix：把 `cas` value 的 leading token 校验纳入 `configCasProblem` 或统一 `configCasState`：存在 live `cas` 且 token 不是 `optional|required` 时返回 problem；`--no-cas` 仍先于该校验。

CCIMPL-2 — live row with inline HTML comment is dropped.  
Spec 说 `|`-leading row 取前两格、extra columns ignored，HTML comment 是 non-content；但 [lib/config.js](/mnt/d/Workbench/misc/apriori-spec-development/lib/config.js:24) 只要一行含 `<!--` 就整行 `continue`。实际 `| cas | optional | <!-- note -->` 解析为空，合法 live row 被吃掉。  
Fix：按字符或 span 处理 HTML comment：保留 opener 前的 live content；comment-only 行仍 inert；未闭合 comment 从 opener 后到 EOF inert。

CCIMPL-3 — gate C7 still consults/prints waiver when there is no unstamped mutation.  
`GT-16` 要求 stamped/ADDED-only change “passes C7 silently”；但 [lib/gate.js](/mnt/d/Workbench/misc/apriori-spec-development/lib/gate.js:219) 先处理 `--no-cas`，且 [lib/gate.js](/mnt/d/Workbench/misc/apriori-spec-development/lib/gate.js:223) 在 `!um.length` 前读取 `configCas`，所以无须消费 CAS 时仍可能输出 waiver。  
Fix：`archived` 后先算 `um`，`!um.length` 直接 pass；只有存在 unstamped mutations 时再处理 `--no-cas`、config problem 和 config waiver。

**Advisories**

当前模板 header `| Field | Value | ... |` 会被 parser 当 unknown key 读入，而不是“header skipped”。由于 unknown keys 不被消费，未计为 open gap；若要让 parser 单测严格贴合 CF prose，可一并扩展 header skip。

**LEDGER DELTA**

| ID | Issue | Risk | Round found | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CCIMPL-1 | `cas` illegal values are treated as no waiver, not config errors. | Bad config is not named as required; archive/gate diagnostics diverge from fail-closed contract. | STEP5·r1 | open |
| CCIMPL-2 | A live config row with an inline HTML comment is skipped entirely. | Valid rows such as `| cas | optional | <!-- note -->` become inert despite first-two-cells semantics. | STEP5·r1 | open |
| CCIMPL-3 | Gate C7 consults and reports CAS waiver state even when no unstamped mutation exists. | Stamped/ADDED-only changes do not pass C7 silently as specified. | STEP5·r1 | open |

VERDICT: 3 issues open
